# The Beyond: picture list v6

813 pictures, written to the owner's v6 rules (art/V6-PICTURE-RULES.md). `asset-list-v6.csv` has every row; `asset-list-v6-todo.csv` has only what has to be made (everything except the patrons and the title screen).

Columns: file, size, subject, group, era, kind, year, hero, prompt, negative, note. The `style` column is gone: the generator adds the fixed tail.

## What changed from v5
- No style, palette, lighting or mood words in any row. Light sources appear only as objects.
- Era and place come from content: three weighted era objects in every row, and the case's country, city and building repeated in every place row.
- Hero subject first, once per picture. Interiors list indoor objects; places have no people; fight backdrops keep the bottom third plain.
- Agent portraits: 120 different outfits (two to three garments and one detail each), every agent portrait ends "face in the upper middle".
- Monsters are written to look like monsters: wrongness in anatomy and materials, "eldritch horror" or "monstrous" in every creature row.
- The old rows for jobs that no longer exist (the generated side jobs and their "threat" monsters) are gone. Death of a Bloodline's places are all listed.
- Every case, person and enemy is new or rewritten: see the group list below.
